# Agent-ready prioritized backlog

## PLAYTRACE-RENDER-01 — recorded gameplay visual replay (P1)

**Status 2026-11-21: design merged (#164, `b3ec13dc`); PTR-R0 + PTR-R1 merged as #167 (`12e43398`), merged, game-feel review pending; R1 follow-ups and the pinned-build recipe merged as #169 (`904184e8`). PTR-R2, PTR-R3 and PTR-R4 are NOT STARTED.** Goal: reconstruct and inspect a real recorded CF02 human game in the existing Bevy `floor01_scene` renderer (Carl/Donut, enemies, camera, HUD, VFX), with play/pause/tick-step, seek across retries and optional exported PNG frames/FFmpeg video. This is an *offline, read-only* viewer; never capture video during normal live play. It addresses the owner's DEMO-02 feedback: Donut barely acts, goblin collision looks good but its effect needs inspection. [Technical contract](../03-tech/playtrace-render-01-visual-replay.md); [agent implementation order](../07-agents/playtrace-render-01-workorder.md).

**Dependencies:** PLAYTRACE PT-A/B/C merged (#143/#156); `src/playtrace/replay.rs::verify_loaded` already replays deterministic simulation but **does not render**. PT-D exporter #161 is merged (`2804ae20`). Existing 2026-11-21 owner sessions were captured with main `0bffd264`; a new renderer build necessarily changes the coarse source fingerprint. R0 must therefore supply a separate explicit **checked cross-build** visual-replay policy that waives only the source-fingerprint predicate and keeps every strict preflight and per-tick check, while preserving the strict `playtrace verify` and PT-D training gates. Donut-tuning PR #165 changes the simulation (companion data is not in the content hash, so divergence is caught per tick); the owner's sessions are therefore viewed on a **sim-pinned replay build**, after a one-off strict `playtrace verify`. **Adopted default PTR-Q16 (#169):** that build is `12e43398` itself (same simulation sources as `0bffd264` plus the viewer; the earlier plan of cherry-picking R0/R1 onto `0bffd264` hit modify/delete conflicts after the squash merge), run with `scripts/replay_session_pinned.sh <commit> <session-id> [args]` (temporary detached worktree, art copied from the main checkout, worktree removed on exit). General rule: build at the newest commit whose simulation sources match the recording's commit, or at the recording commit if it already contains the viewer. Do not claim pixel-identical rendering or full verification of party `ph` until proven. No dependency on ML training, next companion AI revision, #165 or LAB-02.

**Execution sequence:** PTR-R0 Bevy-free `ReplayCursor` refactor of the verifier's `Replayer` (borrows `SceneSim`/`CombatState`, per-tick lifecycle outcome) + strict/checked policies with regression tests → PTR-R1 `floor01_scene/replay_view.rs` viewer, replay CLI, pause/step, native render and trust UI (first useful deliverable) → PTR-R2 restart, seek, episode/event timeline and AI/contact diagnostics → PTR-R3 deterministic per-tick PNG export and optional external FFmpeg wrapper → PTR-R4 same-session human evidence, two-pad/hardware/performance gate. R0+R1 may be grouped in one PR after coordinating file ownership; keep remaining work independently reviewable. **Acceptance:** a real owner-recorded CF02 fight can be watched without gamepad inputs, Donut's observed inactivity and goblin-body impacts can be found by tick, divergent/recovered/unsupported recordings are never silently rendered, existing hashes and normal gameplay unchanged, source recording remains private and untouched. All code stages remain NOT STARTED until PR evidence proves otherwise.

**Merged status and follow-ups (recorded after #167 and #169, speed mode):**

- **#167 (PTR-R0 + PTR-R1, `12e43398`):** `ReplayCursor` (Bevy-free refactor of the verifier's `Replayer`), `ReplayPolicy::{Strict, CheckedCrossBuild}`, the `floor01_scene --replay-session <ID> [--replay-compatibility checked]` viewer (Space/P pause, `.` or Right step, trust banner, one `PLAYTRACE_REPLAY ... result=STRICT|CHECKED|DIVERGED|UNSUPPORTED` line, never a `PLAYTRACE_VERIFY` line), `ci_floor01_combat.sh replay`, evidence `docs/evidence/ptr-r1/`. Independent top-tier review found two fix-now items, fixed in #169. Step 0: all 8 owner sessions pass strict `playtrace verify` (scope full). All 8 replay as `CHECKED` on the sim-pinned build; the longest session (8739 ticks) replays in about 150 s at 1x under Xvfb. No simulation file or golden changed. Not run: real hardware, performance numbers (R4), a real window-manager close.
- **#169 (`904184e8`):** PTR-Q16 pinned-build recipe, `scripts/replay_session_pinned.sh`, and `replay_finish.after(bevy::window::ExitSystems)` so closing the window prints the final result line (ordering is in the schedule; a real WM close is not verified).
- **Follow-ups (not done):** from #167 and #169: **U-1** label crash-recovered (prefix) sessions `STRICT (prefix)` / `CHECKED (prefix)` and stop at the end of the valid record, never DIVERGED; **U-2** trailing control records are applied without a presentation reset; **U-3** duplicate source-to-kit routing; **U-4** `party_fixture` and `combat_lab` sessions report `unsupported_source` (part of R2); **U-5** `resolve_session` tries a path before an id and an `Unsupported` line may echo a raw argument (local stdout only); **U-6** after #165 or #166 land, `0bffd264` sessions show DIVERGED on main at the first differing AI tick (correct fail-closed behaviour; use the pinned build). From the #164 design review: record `companion_profile = PROFILE_HASH` in new sessions' `content_hashes` (checked only when present), record presentation flags as non-hashed `presentation_*` feature flags, plan a separate simulation fingerprint (`src/` minus presentation modules), measure R3 throughput (13k ticks at one tick per image is tens of minutes; export must force CINE slow-mo off), and use the in-crate Rig for CF02 test sessions because `playtrace synth-fixture` refuses arena fixtures. Settings and presentation limits already accepted: presentation flags are not recorded, particles run on wall clock while paused, one animation frame around a restart or respawn may differ from live, `ph` is still not re-derived.
- **Next:** PTR-R2 (in-viewer restart and seek, speed 0.5x/1x/2x, Donut inactivity and goblin-contact event inspection, party-fixture and lab sources), then R3 (deterministic PNG export, optional external FFmpeg), then R4 (same-session human evidence, two-pad, hardware and performance gate).

The likely cause, not separated from where the blink lands, is the pilot's swat-hold rule (it holds swat and pounce whenever a swing aims at her, even outside the 7 to 12 tick window). The boss-charge blink guard is dead in the shipped stack (the jump dodge fires 18 ticks before the ram and the window opens at 12) and its window was not widened.

- **Hazard-room evidence (#231):** the HAZ-B real-window evidence was **NOT RUN**. The room-scene capture path (EVID-ROOM, #233, merged) is HAZ-C's first deliverable; room 4 now has gate fixtures (#240) and a spectacular route clip (#242).

- **BOS-B #331** (`d7cce75f`), merged, game-feel review pending (the Tuskling art is the existing TUSK-01 atlas, no pixel changed):
  - the Ball's Tusklings on ENEMY-SLOT (`src/tuskling.rs`, `src/combat_scene/tuskling.rs`): a crash or a rack pin detaches 2 on the first impact and 1 on each later one, at most 3 live and 5 per attempt; a 20-tick scramble, a 28-tick tell, a 3-tick lunge, a 60-tick cooldown; defeats use `EnemyDefeated` and the new `tuskling` species profile (BOSB-AD1..AD7);

  - on the Ball's defeat live Tusklings cower, harmless, and `f01_ball_defeated` is written once as a combat-log fact (BOSB-AD9, AD11);
  - the bot's alcove-flee while a roll is told or running (BOSB-AD10); the crash costs the Ball 3 HP, never the last HP, through the new `BossSelfHurt` event (BOSB-AD12); the `carl_environment` and `duo_coop` policies as fixtures and `bos_eval` rows G-0e and G-0d (BOSB-AD13);

## Floor 1 completion and the owner playtest (batch 12, 2026-11-27)

**Owner decisions, recorded verbatim with the dates the orchestrator logged them.**

- **2026-11-26, go with all.** "Go with all because I could not test it until next week." Items that were owner-gated (the Light on Your Feet grant, ENG-02, the audience in the run, Act I, the refuge and camp, the troglodytes, Ball of Swine) proceed with adopted defaults recorded by ID; the owner reviews game feel and art next week. Legal, rights and provenance guardrails are unchanged. `ENG2-AD1` and `audience-run-design.md` cite 2026-11-25 for the same sentences; the orchestrator's log (2026-11-26 03:17) is the record.
- **2026-11-26, everything ends on main.** "Ensure that everything ends on main for testing all together next week." No work order ends as an open draft; every room and feature is reachable in the single command `--scene f01_late --f01-run`, new behaviour is on by default where the spec allows it, and a large item that cannot finish is merged as a labelled playable greybox. The owner drafts #179, #187, #189, #191 and #192 stay open; their content lands through new PRs that cite them.
- **2026-11-25, parallelism.** Three agents in parallel, ramping up later within the five-hour limit.
- **2026-11-26, cap of 2 parallel agents.** "Continue, reduce parallelism a bit": at most 2 agents at once, reviewers count.
- **2026-11-27, budget order** (74 % of the weekly limit used): "(1) camp art #308, (2) troglodytes (plan, TROG-A, TROG-B, art), (3) PLAYTEST-READY-01 before the limit runs out; after the reset: Ball of Swine + floor exit (#192), Act I room art (chase/shore/guild), room polish (corral/dozer/hazards/mastery), cleanup." Savings: fix-only rounds get the orchestrator fix check (no top-tier re-look unless geometry or logic changed); the V7 workshop wall and cleanup wait until after the reset.

The owner's entry point for the playtest is [`f01-playtest-ready-01.md`](f01-playtest-ready-01.md) (how to run, the 17 rooms, the checklist, every adopted default, the art notes); its appendix [`f01-playtest-ready-01-adopted-defaults.md`](f01-playtest-ready-01-adopted-defaults.md) lists every adopted-default ID since `bebd5948`. The evidence of this batch is in `docs/evidence/playtest-ready-01/`.

Rooms 1 to 18 are the manifest order of the floor run (the first column's numbers are run positions; older docs use the legacy room labels: the Hoarder is room 1, the Boiler 2, the Dozer 3, the hazards 4, the Juicer 5, the mastery arena 6). Cross-room: since #263 the dynamite blast's drawn size reads as its gameplay radius (`dfx_wave_r64`, `DANGER-FX-DYNAMITE`) in every room where Carl holds dynamite. `docs/03-tech/floor01-scene.md` describes the Hoarder's presentation from the merged code since #265.

## MVP controller grammar — approved design (2026-11-18)

**Owner-approved for MVP:** the four face-button verbs are Cross/A jump, Square/X primary attack, Triangle/Y physical attack (**Carl kicks; never enemy grabs**), and Circle/B story-unlocked special. R1/RB interacts; L1/LB swaps heroes in solo. Donut is an agile low-damage cat before the biscuit and gains Magic Missile as her primary after it; Carl remains physically focused and later uses story-earned explosives. The control grammar is future-floor-extensible, controller-independent and explicitly excludes mandatory twin-stick aim, dodge or sprint inputs. See [the authoritative controller contract](../01-design/mvp-controller-contract.md).

**Implementation state:** partly implemented (I-03 PR A, draft): `floor01_scene` fixtures now have separate PrimaryAttack, PhysicalAttack (Triangle/Y, K / North) and EquippedSpecial inputs, replay-tested, with the old `special_pressed`-as-kick mapping migrated (pad restart moved North to Select, owner sign-off pending). The rest of the contract is **not** implemented/validated in the integrated Bevy scene: interact, solo swap, Steam Controller/Deck mappings and 2P. Still to do: preserve deterministic replay and prove DualSense + Steam Controller/Steam Deck play, true 2P controls, solo swap and pre/post-biscuit capability gates. Game-feel, input timings, aerial move variants and optional aiming/special cycling remain pending human playtests. Avoid parallel modifications to the ongoing combat scene input schema.

## Approved Floor 01 enemy concept batch (2026-11-17)

**Owner approved all visual enemy concepts:** opening common goblins A/B, pot-helmet engineer, Murder Dozer, Bad Llama C+A, Scatterer, Scatterer Brood Guardian, Rot Sticker, Green Slime and The Hoarder. See [the concept roster](../02-art/floor-01-enemy-concept-roster.md) for individual source refs, boundaries and the upload manifest. The five approved concept PNGs have been uploaded to the concept PR and checked against the image manifest; final native pixel masters and gameplay remain separate work. **Do not restart existing goblin/Dozer/scene work**: common goblin A/B native sources are already in main; check current open integration and Dozer PRs before assigning new art agents.

**Later visual approval:** The Hoarder dedicated study, Troglodyte Pygmy/Basher/Virtuoso and The Juicer, plus the Ball of Swine and constituent Tusklings, are recorded in [the supplementary roster](../02-art/floor-01-late-enemy-boss-concepts.md). All three new images are now committed and validated against the eight-entry manifest in the late concept PR; local production agents should not duplicate that conceptual work. These formerly pending Level-2 rats, Goblin Bomb Bard and Goblin Copper Chopper concepts are now owner-approved (see the next paragraph). The optional unseen Goblin Chieftain and standalone Juicer gym and Ball of Swine arena views remain separate potential work.

**Later minor-enemy visual concepts (2026-11-17):** The owner accepted the Dungeon Rat, Goblin Bomb Bard and Goblin Copper Chopper visual directions; see [the approved minor-enemy brief](../02-art/floor-01-minor-enemy-concepts.md). All three original concept PNGs have been committed on their **separate** PR and verified against original Git blob identifiers, SHA-256, sizes and dimensions; the manifest/test now validates eleven images. The Juicer's Gym visual treatment is owner-approved, but its standalone environment concept and reception/turnstile/room layout remain work for a **different** PR. No new combat mechanics or engine assets are authorized by these concept approvals.

No simulation file, golden, `Cargo.toml` or `Cargo.lock` changed. **Real data:** the owner's 5 local `--cf02` sessions (13,579 ticks, 6 episodes, all verified with the `0bffd264` build that recorded them) export 13,579 human Carl rows (train 3 / dev 1 / holdout 1 sessions, 0 leakage) and 13,574 AI Donut transition rows; the BC smoke reaches horizontal accuracy 0.922 against a 0.718 majority baseline on dev. **This proves the pipeline only, not a policy**: one player, one scenario, a few minutes, the same player and scenario across all splits, and a model given the previous 4 actions does worse than repeating the previous action. PT-E (owner capture acceptance and performance evidence, real-pad and 2P sessions, geometry and enemy holdout, reward-profile export) is NOT STARTED. **PT-D follow-ups (U-1..U-8, fix forward):** U-1 the pseudonym split is unused by real manifests and the level variant is not enforced; U-2 `outcome` and `episode_end.episode` are not verified by the replay and a duplicate `episode_end` is not rejected; U-3 no real-verifier coverage of the in-step `post_reset` defeat path (a `synth-fixture` variant that loses the party once); U-4 no segment-end marker for a human-to-AI swap cut; U-5 AI ticks without `eff` are invisible in `stats`; U-6 error details may carry absolute paths into `dataset.json` and the printed summary; U-7 `repo_root()` is fragile for a pip-installed package; U-8 unanchored `dataset-*/` in `.gitignore` and `splitlines()` on U+2028/U+0085. **Owner-approved PR grouping (2026-11-19).** PR #103 includes HUD-DEBT-05. HUD-DEBT-04 and HUD-DEBT-01 go in one PR after #103 merges. HUD-DEBT-02 design goes in PR #107 (adopted 2026-11-19, design only); its implementation is a later PR after HUD-DEBT-04/01 and Q10. HUD-DEBT-06 and HUD-DEBT-07 go in one PR. HUD-DEBT-03 is a separate PR. This grouping supersedes "one PR per item" above.

**Parallelism rule:** run 2 to 3 independent items in parallel, chosen by difficulty (for example a small input item beside a gameplay item, never two items that edit the same gameplay state or the same files); one PR per item; serialize items that share a dependency in the order above.

## Opening-scene combat integration handoff (owner direction, 2026-11-17)

- **Use the first integrated scene as the combat testbed:** [Floor 01 opening combat probes](../07-agents/floor-01-integrated-combat-probes.md). Keep the opening Dozer chase as escape; add the planned small **on-foot goblin fight** after the chase with reproducible CP-01/02/03 fixtures in the **same Bevy runtime**.
- **Critical first tests:** a satisfying ordinary hit/kill, one knockback into another enemy, then an optional single authored environmental elimination if safe and story-valid. Defeated-body impacts (CP-04) are an A/B research spike, not a prerequisite.
- **Integration discipline:** re-use WORK-021's deterministic combat contracts; the current integrated corridor alone is not a combat demo. Preserve pre-Guild ability constraints (no Donut magic, no early Carl bomb simply because greybox has it) and avoid simultaneous writes to scene ownership/colliders.
- **Co-op validation:** run the same battle in solo-switch and 2P when those features are actually available; never mark missing controls as tested or block all basic hit testing on future multiplayer.

## Fun over canon (owner decision, 2026-11-21)

- **Rule (merged #177, `5645de5b`):** gameplay wins when book canon and good gameplay disagree. Timing, numbers, ranges, cooldowns and how an ability behaves are tuned for fun, readability and co-op; every deliberate deviation is recorded in its design doc as a **game adaptation** with a one-line reason and is never presented as canon. See [vision](../01-design/vision.md#fun-over-canon-owner-decision-2026-11-21). Applies to CARL-PROG-01, DONUT-PROG-01, DONUT-MOBILITY-01 and the F01 setpieces.

## Hybrid combat physics and feel (owner approval, 2026-11-17)

- **Approved direction:** [combat-feel and hybrid-physics contract](../01-design/combat-feel-hybrid-physics.md). Ordinary enemies fall after a few satisfying hits, with physical feedback, predictable knockback and emergent enemy/environment interactions rather than memorized combo strings or HP-sponges.
- **Validation proposal:** COMBAT-PHYS-01 in stages: one enemy/direct hit -> knockback into enemy/solid -> one controlled authored environmental defeat -> contrasting Carl/Donut positions and 1P/2P. **No promise that the integrated Floor 1 corridor currently supports combat.**
- **Still unknown:** exact hit count, knockback strength, collision damage for flying/corpse bodies, hitstop length, camera motion, combo scoring, and boss resistance. These require playtests and deterministic Rust+Bevy evidence.

## Combat-first design priority (owner decision, 2026-11-17)

- **Mandatory:** [combat-first encounter rule](../01-design/combat-first-encounter-design.md). A satisfying basic kill, one clearly causal spectacular environmental kill, and a genuine Carl/Donut combat opportunity outrank expanding traversal gates or extra scenic areas. The game is **action first**, not a chain of puzzle rooms. Quiet refuges/chases are deliberate contrasts.
- **Proposed next gameplay review:** a small integrated **COMBAT-FEEL-01** fight with a regular enemy, one breakable environmental hazard and an elevated Donut position, tested in both solo switch and local 2P once two-actor control exists. **No gameplay implementation is claimed here.**
- **Scope:** Don't begin a large combinatorial kill system or huge bestiary until direct-hit feel, readable environmental aftermath and two-character agency have human evidence.

## Floor 01 gameplay/progression and co-op human experiments (2026-11-16)

- **Approved gameplay target:** hybrid solo switching + assisted AI/context orders, and local **two-player Carl + Donut from the first playable moment**, without early-unlocked magical abilities. See [Floor 01 gameplay bible](../01-design/floor-01-gameplay-progression-v0.md).
- **Approved level-authoring rule (2026-11-17):** [asymmetric traversal and one-character gates](../01-design/asymmetric-traversal-and-gates.md). Donut can jump higher/farther; Carl selectively breaks/opens paths. **No requirement for a parallel Carl and Donut route in each room.** Story-critical gates may require either character, solved by swapping in 1P or specialized roles in 2P; protect unlock order, reunion and recoverability. B7/E1 tests a two-gate sequence (Donut ledge -> Carl weak barrier), then later progression develops non-obvious combinations.
- **Not implemented in current Floor 1 scene:** direct Donut control, second-player device ownership, shared two-actor camera, swapping/commands, mounted play, attacks or Goblin Dozer chase. They must not be marked accepted simply because COMP-01/02 rendering CI passed.
- **Owner next:** complete [Stage A of the integrated-scene playtest](../07-agents/floor-01-integrated-scene-playtest.md): Carl movement, cast readability at 640×360/640×400, A1 vs no-lighting, camera and A/B/C pacing; capture three actionable observations.
- **Proposed following isolated work:** WORK-005 second controllable actor/device ownership -> shared duo camera -> WORK-006 solo swap/context AI -> optional SHOULDER-SPIKE-01 transport vs mounted attack -> encounter fixtures. **Shoulder Mode and mounted attacks are brainstorming and not accepted implementations.**
- **Human gate:** a dedicated two-person test validates both roles, hot join/drop, co-op camera and separate vs mounted value before new combat/skill-tree complexity.

## Floor 01 art atlas v1 — area concepts (2026-11-16)

- **Concept direction selected:** [Floor 01 ten-area atlas](../02-art/floor-01-art-atlas-v1.md) with a shared A/B/C catacomb substrate, distinct territories and special rooms. Book-derived attributes are distinguished from artistic interpretation.
- **Agent sequence:** [area-kit plan](../07-agents/floor-01-area-kits-plan.md), beginning **after** the integrated Bevy corridor [COMP-01 PR #29](https://github.com/pando85/dungeon-floorbreak/pull/29) has actual evidence and a human visual/game-feel gate.
- **Concept PNGs committed and verified:** [ten image references (overview + areas 02–10)](../../assets/concept/floor-01/atlas-v1/README.md) and a [SHA-256 manifest](../../assets/concept/floor-01/atlas-v1/manifest.json) are now in the PR; all ten Git blob identities match the original local reference bytes. Area 01 remains in the previously merged [opening concepts](../../assets/concept/floor-01/opening/README.md).
- **Known incompleteness:** Gym and Ball of Swine use **provisional cropped atlas panels** rather than separate full-resolution concept art; neither arena geometry nor sprite output is approved. No new game mechanics are added by this doc PR.

## New art-direction handoff — Floor 01 opening (2026-11-16)

- **Selected:** [opening catacomb + goblin / Murder Dozer concept direction](../02-art/floor-01-opening-catacomb-goblins.md). This locks the A/B/C environment grammar, gradual immersion, visually distinct goblins, and a pursuit-first Dozer encounter. It is **concept acceptance only**.
- **Deferred:** [local-agent implementation handoff](../07-agents/floor-01-opening-encounter-handoff.md). Start environment/sprite/chase work only after the ongoing Bevy Floor 1 greybox PR #21 and Character Playground PR #22 are reviewed/merged and the real 52/40 px character atlases can be composed.
- **Committed reference art:** [clean catacomb, three goblins and Murder Dozer](../../assets/concept/floor-01/opening/README.md) as **three PNG concept references**, with hashes and provenance in the [manifest](../../assets/concept/floor-01/opening/manifest.json). Git blobs were checked against the original review files; not native production sprites.
- **Not delivered:** editable terrain/enemy/Dozer masters, actual Bevy scene, chase logic, canon-accurate goblin pineapple-clubs, or player testing. The full chase illustration with incorrect Carl/Donut remains excluded.

The concept illustration alone does **not** meet its acceptance gate.

## First agent work order (WORK-001)

**Goal:** start a clean, reproducible Rust 2024 Bevy 0.20.x Linux binary with a 2D pixel camera, empty test level, a gamepad-neutral action map and one headless action test. **No character likenesses.**

**Scope:** a small workspace or crate, lockfile, README build/run command, one input module and one CLI/headless test. UI must be controllable with at least one gamepad input adapter or a testable synthetic action stream. Do not integrate Steam SDK, Avian or LDtk until their role is needed.

**Expected acceptance:** `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test`, `cargo run`; include actual test logs and a screenshot from a Linux session. Report skipped GPU/hardware test honestly.

**Notes:** Pin the specific 0.20 patch release after checking current crate metadata; do not blindly copy API examples from older Bevy versions. Obtain human acceptance of pixel scaling before WORK-003.

## WORK-008 execution state

The Aseprite-compatible art pipeline is implemented by **PR #13**, with three
editable original-IP-neutral .ase source fixtures and LibreSprite v1.3 in CI.
The export, metadata checks, nine negative tests and byte-identical repeat
export **passed GitHub Actions**; the 640×360 and 2× contact sheets were
uploaded as CI evidence. This satisfies the **WORK-008 tooling spike**, but
not WORK-007's art quality requirement. A human review of actual native-scale
art is still required before a visual style can be labeled production-ready.
See [asset-pipeline.md](../02-art/asset-pipeline.md).

## WORK-022 — Character Playground (PR #22)

**Scope:** Independent Rust + Bevy 0.20.1 cast-inspection executable; 52 px Carl, 40 px Donut and 76 px adult Mongo at original virtual-pixel scale with four-frame `idle`, pivot-aligned fixed-step movement/jump, keyboard/gamepad selection, and 640×360 / 640×400 camera baselines.

**Evidence target:** `.github/workflows/character-playground.yml` exports existing editable masters, checks generated atlas contracts, runs Rust tests plus a headless smoke, and uploads two true Bevy-rendered Linux screenshots. Follow `docs/03-tech/character-playground.md`.

**Status:** Merged via PR #22 on 2026-11-16. All four workflows passed on the final PR head; real Bevy 640×360 and 640×400 screenshots were reviewed technically. Owner explicitly deferred hands-on gamepad/visual acceptance to the **integrated Floor 1 scene**. Native sizes are approved; locomotion/jump animation, overlapping silhouettes and mounted Donut remain future work. The playground binary is independent of the combat greybox until a separate integration PR.
